FBA reimbursement notification
 You have a reimbursement activity
 Hello from Fulfilment by Amazon,
We have initiated a reimbursement, a reimbursement reversal, or both due to recent activity on your selling account. No action is required from you.
Reimbursements can be initiated for an issue that you notified us about or one that we identified at a fulfilment centre. A reimbursement reversal occurs when a reimbursement decision has been reviewed and changed. Reimbursement amounts are determined in accordance with the FBA inventory reimbursement policy ( https://sellercentral.amazon.co.uk/gp/help/external/200213130 ).
Please note that it may take up to five business days for reimbursement transactions to appear in your seller account.
You can find details on your reimbursement or reversal transaction in the Reimbursements report ( https://sellercentral.amazon.co.uk/reportcentral/REIMBURSEMENTS/0 ) in Seller Central:
- In the Event Date drop-down menu, select a date range and click Generate report . You can use the date in the subject line of this email as a point of reference.
- To identify inventory reimbursements and reversals, see the Quantity reimbursed [inventory] column.

 We have charged your credit card for $88.37 in an attempt to settle your Amazon seller account balance.

												为什么我会收到KYC的资质审核要求？
												KYC（Know Your Customer） 是欧洲站开店卖家的资质审核。
												不同卖家触发KYC审核的节点或有不同，账号注册过程中，或注册完成进入卖家平台后，更改卖家后台关键信息（比如受益人信息/地址信息等）或达到一定的销售额阈值前。 只有通过了资质审核才可以在欧洲站长期销售。

 Hello,
 We are conducting a review of your Amazon Payments Account. We need additional information or documents to continue with your account verification.  What happens next?

 Once the verification is complete, you will receive a performance notification and email advising you of the result.

[Action Required] Information needed for your Selling on Amazon payment account
 [Action Required] Information needed for your Selling on Amazon payment account
 Hello,
We are contacting you because we need additional documentation to continue with your account verification.
Providing altered or manipulated documents may result in permanent account closure.
What happens if I do not take action?
You have a total of 60 days from the day when we first contacted you to take action or provide all of the requested documentation and information to complete the verification. The time that we take to review and validate the outcomes of your action or validate the information will not be counted against the 60-day threshold that you are given.
If you do not take action or provide requested information before these 60 days, your listings and the ability to disburse funds will be temporarily deactivated until verification has been completed.
If it has been more than 60 days since we first contacted you, your disbursements and listings might have already been deactivated and will remain deactivated until the requested information or documentation has been provided and your account has been v

Global Warehousing and Distribution’s new China shipping options are live
时间分组：last7 邮箱：account_4 发件人：Global Warehousing and Distribution <donotreply@amazon.com> 日期：Thu, 16 Jul 2026 11:19:50 +0000 命中词： 抓取方式：RETR 检测时间：2026-07-17 14:44:03
展开查看邮件预览 
96
 Choose Shanghai or Shenzhen with Free on Board support
 Global Warehousing and Distribution (GWD)
 GWD now gives you more flexibility in how you ship to US FBA. GWD has expanded to Shanghai, one of China's largest manufacturing and export hubs, and now supports the Free on Board (FOB) incoterm across all its locations.
 What this means for you:
- Send to a location closer to your manufacturer: Choose from GWD facilities in Shanghai or Shenzhen to shorten the distance between your manufacturer and GWD, so your inventory is received and ready for US FBA replenishment faster.
- Keep control of your export documentation: With the FOB incoterm, you can obtain separate invoices for export declaration and origin port handling charges to support your export tax rebate process.
- Reduce US storage costs: Store bulk inventory with GWD in Shanghai or Shenzhen and move it into US FBA based on demand, so you're not paying US storage fees on inventory that isn't ready to sell.
- Start small, scale when ready: Send as little as one shipment to GWD, with no long-term commitment and no peak storage fee increases, so you can confirm GWD fits your supply chain before increasing volume.

FBA reimbursement notification
 You have a reimbursement activity
 Hello from Fulfilment by Amazon,
We have initiated a reimbursement, a reimbursement reversal, or both due to recent activity on your selling account. No action is required from you.
Reimbursements can be initiated for an issue that you notified us about or one that we identified at a fulfilment centre. A reimbursement reversal occurs when a reimbursement decision has been reviewed and changed. Reimbursement amounts are determined in accordance with the FBA inventory reimbursement policy ( https://sellercentral.amazon.co.uk/gp/help/external/200213130 ).
Please note that it may take up to five business days for reimbursement transactions to appear in your seller account.
You can find details on your reimbursement or reversal transaction in the Reimbursements report ( https://sellercentral.amazon.co.uk/reportcentral/REIMBURSEMENTS/0 ) in Seller Central:
- In the Event Date drop-down menu, select a date range and click Generate report . You can use the date in the subject line of this email as a point of reference.
- To identify inventory reimbursements and reversals, see the Quantity reimbursed [inventory] column.
-

 You have a reimbursement activity
 Hello from Fulfilment by Amazon,
We have initiated a reimbursement, a reimbursement reversal, or both due to recent activity on your selling account. No action is required from you.
Reimbursements can be initiated for an issue that you notified us about or one that we identified at a fulfilment centre. A reimbursement reversal occurs when a reimbursement decision has been reviewed and changed. Reimbursement amounts are determined in accordance with the FBA inventory reimbursement policy ( https://sellercentral.amazon.co.uk/gp/help/external/200213130 ).
Please note that it may take up to five business days for reimbursement transactions to appear in your seller account.
You can find details on your reimbursement or reversal transaction in the Reimbursements report ( https://sellercentral.amazon.co.uk/reportcentral/REIMBURSEMENTS/0 ) in Seller Central:
- In the Event Date drop-down menu, select a date range and click Generate report . You can use the date in the subject line of this email as a point of reference.
- To identify inventory reimbursements and reversals, see the Quantity reimbursed [inventory] column.
-

 [Action Required] Information needed for your Selling on Amazon payment account
 Hello,
We are contacting you because we need additional documentation to continue with your account verification.
Providing altered or manipulated documents may result in permanent account closure.
What happens if I do not take action?
You have a total of 60 days from the day when we first contacted you to take action or provide all of the requested documentation and information to complete the verification. The time that we take to review and validate the outcomes of your action or validate the information will not be counted against the 60-day threshold that you are given.
If you do not take action or provide requested information before these 60 days, your listings and the ability to disburse funds will be temporarily deactivated until verification has been completed.

完成账户验证以恢复付款
时间分组：older 邮箱：account_4 发件人：亚马逊开店服务 <donotreply@amazon.com> 日期：Fri, 10 Jul 2026 12:07:00 +0000 命中词： 抓取方式：RETR 检测时间：2026-07-17 14:43:38
展开查看邮件预览 
96
 完成账户验证以恢复付款
 尊敬的 Weihai EU：
您好！
 您的资金被扣留，因为您的 Amazon.de 卖家账户正在审核中，需要其他验证信息。如果验证未完成，您的账户将被停用。
 在此过程中，资金将保留在您的账户中，但无法提现。提交所需信息以重新激活提现并避免对您的账户产生进一步影响。
 为什么会发生这种情况？
 我们采取此措施是因为，要继续验证您的账户，您需要提供其他验证详情。此验证有助于确保商城诚信并保护买家。我们还会定期审核账户信息，以确保相关信息始终为最新信息。
 要了解更多信息，请参阅“ Amazon Payments -‘我要开店’用户协议 ”。
 我们综合利用多种自动化方式做出这一决定。
 如何完成验证？
 请完成以下步骤以验证您的账户:
- 点击“优先操作”部分中的“提交验证”，访问 账户状况页面 ，或前往卖家平台的 身份信息 部分
- 按照提示提供所需信息/文件
- 点击“保存”，然后点击“提交以进行验证”
 显示的横幅将显示所需文件的相关信息。
 注意： 只有具有“确认/更改账户信息”权限的用户可以提交验证。请仅使用拉丁字符输入所有地址。
 如果我不完成验证，会怎么样？
 如果您未在 57 天内完成验证:
- 您的付款将保持暂停状态
- 您在 Amazon.de 上的所有现有商品将被停售
- 您无法创建新商品
 验证完成之前，这些限制将一直有效。
 我们愿意随时为您提供帮助。
 如果您对此流程有任何疑问，请联系 销售伙伴支持 。
 Amazon Payments Europe S.C.A. (société en commandite par actions) 是一家股份有限合伙公司，注册于卢森堡，注册号 (RCS Luxembourg) B 153 265，公司办公地址为 38 avenue J.F.Kennedy, L-1855 Luxembourg。增值税税号为 LU 24448288。Amazon Payments Europe S.C.A 经 Commission de Surveillance du Secteur Financier 授权为电子货币机构（许可证号 36/10）。Amazon Payments 是 Amazon Payments Europe S.C.A. 的商号。

 Completa la verificación de la cuenta para restaurar los pagos
 Hola, Weihai EU:
 Los fondos están retenidos porque tu cuenta de vendedor de Amazon.es está en revisión y necesitamos información adicional para llevar a cabo la verificación. Si no se completa la verificación, se desactivará tu cuenta.
 Durante este proceso, los fondos permanecerán en tu cuenta, pero no se podrán desembolsar. Presenta la información obligatoria para reactivar los pagos y evitar una mayor repercusión en tu cuenta.

 Do podjęcia tej decyzji wykorzystaliśmy połączenie zautomatyzowanych środków.

- Przejdź na stronę Kondycja konta , klikając „Prześlij do weryfikacji” w

完成账户验证以恢复付款
时间分组：older 邮箱：account_4 发件人：亚马逊开店服务 <donotreply@amazon.com> 日期：Fri, 10 Jul 2026 11:44:42 +0000 命中词： 抓取方式：RETR 检测时间：2026-07-17 14:43:33
展开查看邮件预览 
96
 完成账户验证以恢复付款
 尊敬的 Weihai EU：
您好！
 您的资金被扣留，因为您的 Amazon.ie 卖家账户正在审核中，需要其他验证信息。如果验证未完成，您的账户将被停用。
 在此过程中，资金将保留在您的账户中，但无法提现。提交所需信息以重新激活提现并避免对您的账户产生进一步影响。
 为什么会发生这种情况？
 我们采取此措施是因为，要继续验证您的账户，您需要提供其他验证详情。此验证有助于确保商城诚信并保护买家。我们还会定期审核账户信息，以确保相关信息始终为最新信息。
 要了解更多信息，请参阅“ Amazon Payments -‘我要开店’用户协议 ”。
 我们综合利用多种自动化方式做出这一决定。
 如何完成验证？
 请完成以下步骤以验证您的账户:
- 点击“优先操作”部分中的“提交验证”，访问 账户状况页面 ，或前往卖家平台的 身份信息 部分
- 按照提示提供所需信息/文件
- 点击“保存”，然后点击“提交以进行验证”
 显示的横幅将显示所需文件的相关信息。
 注意： 只有具有“确认/更改账户信息”权限的用户可以提交验证。请仅使用拉丁字符输入所有地址。
 如果我不完成验证，会怎么样？
 如果您未在 57 天内完成验证:
- 您的付款将保持暂停状态
- 您在 Amazon.ie 上的所有现有商品将被停售
- 您无法创建新商品
 验证完成之前，这些限制将一直有效。
 我们愿意随时为您提供帮助。
 如果您对此流程有任何疑问，请联系 销售伙伴支持 。
 Amazon Payments Europe S.C.A. (société en commandite par actions) 是一家股份有限合伙公司，注册于卢森堡，注册号 (RCS Luxembourg) B 153 265，公司办公地址为 38 avenue J.F.Kennedy, L-1855 Luxembourg。增值税税号为 LU 24448288。Amazon Payments Europe S.C.A 经 Commission de Surveillance du Secteur Financier 授权为电子货币机构（许可证号 36/10）。Amazon Payments 是 Amazon Payments Europe S.C.A. 的商号。

 Hello,
 According to rules for online stores issued by the State Council of China (Decree No. 810), we are required to provide information on China-based sellers to the Chinese tax authority on a quarterly basis.
 Some sellers identified potential data discrepancies with the first quarterly tax report covering January to March 2026. Our team has carefully reviewed all reporting to ensure it accurately reflects your activity in our store and resubmitted Q1 2026 reports to the Chinese tax authority where necessary.

RE:[CASE 12309992722] 需要采取行动：请向亚马逊提交您的EPR包装法注册号
【PPWR法规8月12日生效倒计时两个月合规提醒】
卖家您好，系统数据显示您可能尚未完成PPWR合规。提醒您务必尽快开始行动。如果您被认定为不合规，根据国家/地区不同，亚马逊可能需要停售您的不合规商品。
【您需要完成的事项】
1. 优先完成ES、IE、PL、SE站点的包装法号码注册和上传。上传路径：绩效 → 账户状况 → 监管合规性 → 点击右上角【提交合规信息】→ 选择"EPR合规-包装" → 上传号码。
2. 西班牙包装法注册需要西班牙本地税号，您目前可能尚未持有。请立刻开始注册流程，以确保尽快完成合规。

- 服务商网络PPWR专属页面： https://spn.globalsellingcommunity.cn/dynamicMarketingLanding?id=16&launchPositionCode=5PCR95
- 通过合规服务商城寻找服务提供商为您完成 ERN 号码注册以及后续申报缴费: https://sellercentral-europe.amazon.com/service-hub?ref=CNPPWR
- PPWR合规操作手册： https://m.media-amazon.com/images/G/01/CN_Compliance_Team/PPWR_Compliance_Guidebook.pdf

96
 Choose Shanghai or Shenzhen with Free on Board support
 Global Warehousing and Distribution (GWD)
 GWD now gives you more flexibility in how you ship to US FBA. GWD has expanded to Shanghai, one of China's largest manufacturing and export hubs, and now supports the Free on Board (FOB) incoterm across all its locations.
 What this means for you:
- Send to a location closer to your manufacturer: Choose from GWD facilities in Shanghai or Shenzhen to shorten the distance between your manufacturer and GWD, so your inventory is received and ready for US FBA replenishment faster.
- Keep control of your export documentation: With the FOB incoterm, you can obtain separate invoices for export declaration and origin port handling charges to support your export tax rebate process.
- Reduce US storage costs: Store bulk inventory with GWD in Shanghai or Shenzhen and move it into US FBA based on demand, so you're not paying US storage fees on inventory that isn't ready to sell.
- Start small, scale when ready: Send as little as one shipment to GWD, with no long-term commitment and no peak storage fee increases, so you can confirm GWD fits your supply chain before increasing volume.

 Activeer je EU-productvermeldingen opnieuw - VAPV-naleving vereist
 Hallo Weihai EU,
 Sommige van je productvermeldingen zijn gedeactiveerd in onze stores van de Europese Unie (EU) omdat er nalevingsinformatie ontbreekt. Deze actie heeft geen invloed op je accountstatus.
 Onderaan deze e-mail vind je voorbeelden van je gedeactiveerde productvermeldingen. De volledige lijst staat op je pagina Accountstatus. Bekijk hieronder de betroffen productvermeldingen en de stappen die je moet volgen om te voldoen aan de vereisten van de Verordening algemene productveiligheid (VAPV).
 Nalevingsinformatie indienen
 Nalevingsinformatie indienen
 Hoe activeer ik mijn productvermeldingen opnieuw?
 Als je je productvermeldingen opnieuw wilt activeren, moet je de vereiste nalevingsinformatie met betrekking tot de VAPV verstrekken door de onderstaande instructies te volgen:
-
 Ga naar je pagina Accountstatus .
- Selecteer ‘Filters toevoegen’ en vink ‘Verwijderde productvermelding’ aan in de kolom ‘Filteren op ondernomen actie’.
- Klik op ‘Indienen’ naast de gedeactiveerde productvermelding en volg de instructies.

 Gå till sidan Kontostatus .
- Välj Lägg till filter och markera Produktpost borttagen i kolumnen Filtrera efter vidtagen åtgärd.
- Klicka på Skicka in bredvid den inaktiverade produktposten och följ instruktionerna.

 Inviare le informazioni sulla conformità
 Inviare le informazioni sulla conformità
 Come faccio a riattivare le mie offerte?
 Per riattivare le offerte, fornisci le informazioni richieste relative alla conformità al regolamento sulla sicurezza generale dei prodotti seguendo le istruzioni riportate di seguito:
-
 Vai alla pagina Stato dell'account .
- Seleziona "Aggiungi filtri" e quindi "Offerta rimossa" nella colonna "Filtra per azione intrapresa".
- Clicca su "Invia" accanto all'offerta disattivata e segui le istruzioni fornite.
- Segui la procedura indi

 Réactivez vos mises en vente dans l’UE – Conformité au RSGP requise
 Bonjour Weihai EU,
 Certaines de vos mises en vente ont été désactivées de nos boutiques en Union européenne (UE) en raison d’informations de conformité manquantes. Cette action n’affecte pas l’état de votre compte.
 Vous trouverez des exemples de vos mises en vente désactivées à la fin de cet e-mail, et la liste complète sur votre page État du compte. Passez en revue les mises en vente concernées et les étapes à suivre pour respecter les exigences du Règlement relatif à la sécurité générale des produits (RSGP) ci-dessous.
 Soumettre des informations de conformité
 Soumettre des informations de conformité
 Comment réactiver mes produits mis en vente ?
 Pour réactiver vos mises en vente, fournissez les informations de conformité pour la RSGP requises en suivant les instructions ci-dessous :
-
 Accédez à votre page État du compte .
- Cliquez sur « Ajouter des filtres » et cochez « Mise en vente supprimée » dans la colonne « Filtrer par action prise ».
- Cliquez sur « Soumettre » à côté du produit mis en vente désactivé et suivez les instructions fournies.
- Suivez les étapes de la section Mise en vente supprimée

Your Amazon.nl Creditnota Fulfillment by Amazon for 6/2026
 96
Beste Shen zhen shi wei hai zhong xin ke ji you xian,
We sturen je dit bericht om je te laten weten dat je elektronische Creditnota Fulfillment by Amazon voor de maand 6 2026 nu beschikbaar is in je Seller Central-account.
Als je toegang wilt krijgen tot je creditnota, ga je naar je Seller Central-account > Overzichten > Bibliotheek met belastingdocumenten > Belastingfacturen voor kosten verkoper.
In je Seller Central-account kun je je creditnota bekijken, downloaden of afdrukken.
Als je nog vragen hebt, kun je contact opnemen met de Ondersteuning voor verkopers.
Met vriendelijke groeten,
Amazon.nl
 Rapporteer een probleem met deze e-mail
 Als je vragen hebt, ga je naar: Seller Central
 Om je e-mailvoorkeuren te wijzigen, ga je naar: Voorkeuren voor meldingen
 We hopen dat je iets aan dit bericht hebt gehad. Als je in de toekomst echter liever niet meer van dit soort e-mails van Amazon.com wilt ontvangen, kun je je afmelden hier.

Your Amazon.se Faktura Fraktas Av Amazon for 6/2026
 96
Hej Shen zhen shi wei hai zhong xin ke ji you xian!
Vi skriver för att informera dig om att din elektroniska Faktura Fraktas Av Amazon för månaden 6/2026 nu finns tillgänglig på ditt Seller Central-konto.
För att komma åt ditt fakturagår du till ditt Seller Central-konto > Rapporter > Bibliotek för skattedokument > Skattefakturor för säljaravgifter.
På ditt Seller Central-konto kan du se, ladda ner eller skriva ut ditt faktura.
Om du har några frågor, kontakta säljarsupport.

Amazon.se

Your VAT invoice for EPR Pay on Behalf charges for Amazon.co.uk sales
时间分组：older 邮箱：account_4 发件人：Amazon Seller Central <donotreply@amazon.com> 日期：Mon, 6 Jul 2026 15:46:03 +0000 命中词： 抓取方式：RETR 检测时间：2026-07-17 14:42:48
展开查看邮件预览 
Your VAT invoice for EPR Pay on Behalf charges for Amazon.co.uk sales
 96
 Hello,
Your invoice for Amazon.co.uk Extended Producer Responsibility (EPR) Pay on Behalf charges is now available.
The invoice includes (1) the eco-contribution fees that Amazon paid on your behalf for sales of products subject to EPR obligations on Amazon.co.uk during the applicable reporting period, and (2) EPR Pay on Behalf service fees for the EPR categories specified in the category-level and ASIN-level summary.
To access your EPR Pay on Behalf invoice , open the attachment in this email or go to Seller Fee Invoices and search for "EPR Pay on Behalf" in the 'Invoice Type' column.
For a category-level summary of the fees charged, applicable reporting periods, and EPR categories, go to Transaction View with Service Fees as Transaction type, and search for "EPR" in the "Product Details" column.
For an ASIN-level summary of your EPR eco-contribution fees, applicable reporting periods, and EPR categories and sub-categories, go to EPR Category Reports .
 Why was I charged?

Presentar información de cumplimiento normativo de la RAP en Portugal
时间分组：older 邮箱：account_4 发件人：Amazon Services <donotreply@amazon.com> 日期：Wed, 1 Jul 2026 16:04:35 +0000 命中词： 抓取方式：RETR 检测时间：2026-07-17 14:42:33
展开查看邮件预览 
96
 La normativa portuguesa sobre la RAP se aplica a los productos vendidos a clientes de Portugal.
 Hola,
Para cumplir la legislación portuguesa sobre la responsabilidad ampliada del productor (RAP) , tienes que obtener números de registro de responsabilidad ampliada del productor para los productos que vendas a clientes de Portugal.
La RAP es un principio de política medioambiental que hace que el productor sea responsable de los productos a lo largo de todo su ciclo de vida. Esto incluye todo, desde el diseño del producto hasta la gestión al final de su vida útil, como la recogida, clasificación y reciclaje de residuos.
Por lo general, se entiende por "productor" a la parte que comercializa por primera vez en un país un producto sujeto a la normativa RAP.
Tienes que presentarnos los números de registro de la RAP para las siguientes categorías de productos reguladas:
- Embalaje
- Pilas y baterías Para presentarnos tu número de registro de la normativa y demostrar su cumplimiento normativo, sigue los pasos que se indican a continuación:
- En tu cuenta de Seller Central de España, ve a  Estado de la cuenta .
- Selecciona  Cumplimiento normativo .
- Selecciona  Enviar información so

96
 Ponownie przesyłamy kwartalny raport podatkowy, który obejmuje dane takie jak informacje o tożsamości, liczba transakcji, przychody oraz prowizje i opłaty za usługi.
 Dzień dobry,
 23 czerwca 2026 r. po weryfikacji ewentualnych rozbieżności w danych wysłaliśmy zaktualizowany kwartalny raport podatkowy dotyczący Chin za okres od października do grudnia 2025 r.
 Jeśli pobranie raportu nie było możliwe, przesyłamy go ponownie. Poniższe łącza są aktywne przez pięć dni.
 Pobierz kwartalny raport

 Mogą wystąpić rozbieżności między danymi w tych raportach a raportem o płatnościach oraz raportem Amazon o transakcjach podlegających VAT. Każdy raport wykorzystuje odrębną metodologię i służy do innego celu.

 [Amazon.co.uk] One or More of Your Amazon Listings Have Been Search Suppressed
 One or more of your Amazon UK listings have been suppressed from search results
 Amazon took action on one or more of your listings. 1 listing(s) were quarantined and removed from search results on the Amazon website. The affected listings do not meet Amazon’s ASIN Creation Policy , Duplicate ASIN Creation Policy , Product Image Requirements or Product Title Requirements .
 Review and edit quarantined listings Affected Listings
 ASIN SKU Enforcement Reason B0H11DGDRL WH-DE-0508-HCGD-02 More Information Required
 How do I resolve quarantined listings?
 Review and edit the listing(s) on the Suppressed Inventory page in Seller Central to correct the affected attributes or images.
 Once the affected attributes or images have been corrected, the listing(s) will be removed from quarantine and made available in search results.
